`grad_weighted_fast(h,n,p)`:
 p assumed sum 1. dx=2/n; W=p*dx.
 # A via cumsum
 cumW = np.empty(len(W)+1); cumW[0]=0; np.cumsum(W, out=cumW[1:])
 starts = n-1 - np.arange(n) # int
 ends = 2*n - 1 - np.arange(n)
 A = cumW[ends] - cumW[starts]
 # B_minus = convolve(h, W) slice
 v1 = np.convolve(h, W, mode='full')[n-1:2*n-1]
 v2 = np.convolve(h, W[::-1], mode='full')[n-1:2*n-1]
 grad = A - v1 - v2
 return grad

Let's write functions:

```
def feasible_project(h, n):
    target = n/2.0
    h = np.clip(h.copy(),0,1)
    # iterative waterfill to exact target
    for _ in range(100):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
       if diff>0:
           room=1-h
           tot=room.sum()
           if tot<1e-15: break
           # but adding diff*tot may hit 1; if diff<=tot? since target sum and max n, if h sum below target, diff <= tot if max sum n and target <= n. yes.
           h += (diff/tot)*room
       else:
           tot=h.sum()
           if tot<1e-15: break
           h += (diff/tot)*h
    return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
But iterative proportional scaling can be slow; okay. To get sum closer, after loop do `h *= target/h.sum()` if not at bounds? But may violate bounds. Use final clip; sum maybe off. Could use scipy optimize? Maybe enough. For exact sum, we can do **exact bisection scaling**? h = 0.5 + scaling? Not.

Now pair update function:
```
def pair_trial_max(c, h, i, j, delta):
    if delta == 0: return cmax
    ct = c.copy()
    # q=i eps=delta, q=j eps=-delta
    apply_single(ct,h,i,delta)
    apply_single(ct,h,j,-delta)
    s=abs(i-j)
    if s>0:
       ct[n-1] -= 2*delta*delta
       ct[n-1+s] += delta*delta
       ct[n-1-s] += delta*delta
    return float(ct.max())
```
But `apply_single` with h old; if we call for i then j, h unchanged. Good.
Need n from len. Implement with local variables for speed:
```
def _add_single(ct,h,q,eps,n):
    if eps == 0: return
    ct[n-1-q:2*n-1-q] += eps*(1.0-h)
    ct[q:n+q] += -eps*h_rev
```
Need h_rev = h[::-1] (a view). But if h changes? fine. Slices length n. However for q near? q in 0..n-1; start/end valid? For q=0 first start n-1, end 2n-1 (L=2n-1, end exclusive 2n-1 equals len) length n. second start 0, end n. Good. q=n-1 first start 0, end n; second start n-1, end 2n-1. Good.
This adds contributions to same ct. Then cross.
Important: The linear update coefficients use h old. If i and j same? we won't. If q=i and q=j, cross offdiag uses s.

When we accept a move, we need c updated exactly. We can call `pair_apply_c(h,c,i,j,delta)` which modifies c in-place using the same formula, then update h. But careful: If we modify c in-place using old h, then update h, okay. However linear slice add uses h old. If we update h before adding, wrong. So:
```
def pair_apply(h,c,i,j,delta):
   n=len(h)
   apply_single(c,h,i,delta)
   apply_single(c,h,j,-delta)
   s=abs(i-j)
   c[n-1] -=2*delta*delta
   c[n-1+s]+=delta*delta; c[n-1-s]+=delta*delta
   h[i]+=delta; h[j]-=delta
```
But if `apply_single` uses h old and h is being mutated after, okay. If i and j overlap? no.

def pair_line_search(h,c,current_max, i,j, suggest_positive=True):
   maxpos = min(1-h[i], h[j]) if i+... else ...
   if suggest_positive: max_step=maxpos else -maxneg...
   # We might want both directions based on gradient sign.
   # Generate steps: fractions of abs_max_step.
   best = current_max; best_d=0.0
   for frac in [1.0,0.8,0.5,0.3,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0003,0.0001]:
      d=sign*min(frac*maxabs, maxfeas)
      if abs(d)<1e-14: continue
      # also maybe fractions of maxabs or of scale 0.1
      cnew=pair_trial_max(...)
      if cnew<best-1e-15: best=cnew; best_d=d
   # maybe refine around best_d with small neighborhood
   if best_d!=0:
       for mult in [0.5,1.5,0.75,1.25,0.9,1.1]:
          d=clamp(best_d*mult)
          cnew=...
          if cnew<best: update
   return best_d, best
```

if n <= 250:
   diffs = g[ :, None] - g[None, :]? For move from j to i: value = g[j]-g[i]; i row.

Let's detail LBFGS objective/gradient with feasibility.

`_smooth_obj(x,n,alpha,mu)`:
```
dx=2/n
c=np.correlate(x,1-x,'full')*dx
cmax=np.max(c)
sh=alpha*(c-cmax)
# if cmax? stable
logsum = _logsumexp_1d(sh)
val = cmax + logsum/alpha
val += mu*(x.sum()-n/2.0)**2
return val
```
But if x slightly out of bounds? L-BFGS respects bounds. Clip? If line search tries outside, bounds prevent. For safety, `x=np.clip(x,0,1)` in obj? But gradient mismatch if clip. Don't.

`_smooth_grad(x,n,alpha,mu)`:
```
c=...; p=softmax(alpha*c); g=grad_weighted(x,n,p); g += 2mu*(x.sum()-n/2)*ones; return g
```
`p = np.exp(sh - sh.max()); p /= p.sum()`.

Need if alpha=0? Then p uniform. But we use >0.

def candidate_moves(n,g,max_cand,rng):
    moves=[]
    if n <= 400:
       val = g[None, :] - g[:, None]  # (n,n), val[i,j]=g[j]-g[i]
       np.fill_diagonal(val, -np.inf)
       flat = val.ravel()
       if max_cand >= flat.size:
          cand = np.flatnonzero(flat > 0)
       else:
          part = np.argpartition(flat, -max_cand)[-max_cand:]
          cand = part[np.argsort(flat[part])[::-1]]
       for idx in cand: i=idx//n; j=idx%n; if feasible? add
    else:
       # sample
       num_sample = min(100000, max(max_cand*10, 5000))
       aa = rng.randint(0,n,num_sample); bb=rng.randint(0,n,num_sample); mask=aa!=bb
       val = g[bb[mask]] - g[aa[mask]]
       # positive
       pos = val > 0
       if pos.sum() < max_cand: cand_idx = np.where(pos)[0]
       else: top = np.argpartition(val, -max_cand)[-max_cand:]; order...
       moves from aa,bb
    return list of (i,j)
```

Let's design `project_feasible` more robust and exact. Since pair moves maintain sum exactly (floating addition may drift), and LBFGS maybe. We can use a simple projection to [0,1] with sum target via water-filling:

```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    target = n / 2.0
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    # If sum already close return
    for _ in range(1000):
       s = h.sum()
       diff = target - s
       if abs(diff) < 1e-13: break
       if diff > 0:
          room = 1.0 - h
          total = room.sum()
          if total < 1e-15: break
          # if diff > total? shouldn't if target <= n, but due h sum below target and room maybe small. We can distribute with clipping.
          # Use iterative: add to room uniformly, but may exceed 1. Since diff <= total? target <= n, s < target <= n - total? Actually total = n-s, diff=target-s <= n-s? target<=n yes. diff/total <=1. Adding diff/total*room <=1 if room>0. Good.
          h += (diff/total)*room
       else:
          total = s
          if total < 1e-15: break
          h += (diff/total)*h
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    # Final correction: adjust a free coordinate
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
       free = (h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12)
       if free.any():
          h[np.argmax(h*0 + free?)]? Choose index with max room if diff>0 else min h.
          idx = int(np.argmax(np.where(diff>0, 1-h, h)))
          add = np.clip(diff, -(h[idx]), 1-h[idx])
          h[idx] += add
    return h
```
But `np.argmax(np.where(diff>0,1-h,h))` top-level okay. This final may make sum closer but not exact. We can do a few Newton-style: after clip, if diff nonzero, adjust multiple free values minimally:
```
for _ in range(10):
 diff=...
 if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
 if diff>0:
   order? choose elements <1 sorted by room? Too heavy.
   steps = diff / h.sum()? 
```
Maybe enough. constraint tolerance likely 1e-8.

We can make sum exactly using integer counts? If h values floats, exact equality in numpy not possible for many. But if we scale parent? The harness likely uses tolerance. The problem statement says equivalently sum(h) == n_points/2 exactly maybe for construction; but evaluation may not enforce. Use parent sum exact? Previous parent likely generated with sum exact. Our LBFGS may off. We can do a better projection using sorting:

The Euclidean projection onto box with equality can be computed by finding λ such that sum(clip(x-λ,0,1))=target. Monotone. Use bisection on λ. This is better than iterative. Let's implement:
```
def project_feasible(x,n):
    target=n/2.0
    x=np.asarray(x,float)
    lo = x.min() - 1e-12
    hi = x.max() + 1e-12
    # projection p = clip(x - lam,0,1) ; as lam increases, p decreases. Need find lam.
    def _sump(lam): return np.clip(x-lam,0,1).sum()
    for 80 bisect:
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       if _sump(mid) > target: # need decrease sum -> increase lam
          lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    p=np.clip(x-(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
    diff=target-p.sum()
    # adjust free
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
       if diff>0: idx=np.argmax(1-p); add=min(diff,1-p[idx]); p[idx]+=add
       else: idx=np.argmin(p); add=max(diff,-p[idx]); p[idx]+=add
    return p
```
This minimizes distance and gives sum accurate. Bisection 80 * O(n) with clip arrays (80*n) maybe heavy if called often. For n=300, 24k ops, okay. For n=1000, 80k, okay maybe many calls. 80 loops in Python overhead maybe 0.001s. We can reduce to 60. But `np.clip` allocations 60 times, for n=1000 60k elements, 60MB, okay. Projection called after each LBFGS candidate (few) and final, not per trial. Pair search no projection. So okay.
But if x sum already feasible and within bounds, this projection may change h slightly altering objective. To avoid unnecessary distortion, if `np.all((x>=-1e-12)&(x<=1+1e-12))` and abs(x.sum()-target)<1e-10, just clip and return. Do that.
Bisection λ bounds: p(λ)=sum clip(x-λ). If target between sum max/min? x may have sum far. λ range maybe [-1,1]? Since x clipped box, x in [0,1]; projection λ could be outside [0,1]? If target >n? no. λ in [min-1, max+1]. Use lo=-2, hi=2? For x in [0,1], λ between -inf and inf; to increase sum (target > sum x), λ negative. Bound [-1,1] enough? If x all 1 and target n/2, need λ=0.5; if x all 0 and target n/2, λ=-0.5. If x outside, clip? We can do x=np.clip(x,0,1) before? That changes distance but okay. Use lo=-1.0, hi=1.0 after clipping. If target not reached due to flat, adjust. Good.
This projection not preserving parent exact zeros/ones? It may shift all values by λ if no bounds; if sum off by small, λ small. Good.

Define:
```
def project_feasible(x,n):
   x=np.asarray(x,float).copy()
   target=n/2.0
   if np.any(x<0) or np.any(x>1): x=np.clip(x,0,1)
   s=x.sum()
   if abs(s-target) < 1e-12:
       return x
   x=np.clip(x,0,1)
   lo=-1.0; hi=1.0
   # If target close to n? hi? p(1.0)=sum(max(x-1,0))=0; p(-1)=n. target between 0,n.
   for _ in range(60):
      mid=(lo+hi)/2
      psum = np.clip(x-mid,0.0,1.0).sum()
      if psum > target: lo=mid
      else: hi=mid
   lam=(lo+hi)/2
   p=np.clip(x-lam,0.0,1.0)
   diff=target-p.sum()
   if diff > 0:
      idx=int(np.argmax((1.0-p)))
      p[idx]=min(1.0,p[idx]+diff)
   elif diff <0:
      idx=int(np.argmin(p))
      p[idx]=max(0.0,p[idx]+diff)
   return p
```
If diff larger than room of one, may violate; but after bisection residual tiny. Good.

However bisection with x clipped to [0,1] and target=n/2 exactly, λ near 0 if sum half. For L-BFGS infeasible slightly, good.

def check_gradient_formula():

  h2=h.copy(); eps=1e-6
  for idx in [0,7,15]:
     h2[idx]+=eps; c2=c5_full(h2,n); f2=...
     h2[idx]-=2eps; c3=...; f3=...
     fd=(f2-f3)/(2eps)
     if abs(fd-g[idx])>1e-4*(1+abs(fd)): return False
  return True
```

`grad_slow` implementation from previous but with dx and mapping:
```
def grad_weighted_slow(h,n,p):
  dx=2.0/n; L=2*n-1; grad=np.zeros(n)
  for m in range(L):
     wm=p[m]*dx
     if wm < 1e-300: continue
     k=m-(n-1)
     if k>=0:
       nv=n-k
       grad[0:nv] += wm*(1.0-h[k:n])
       grad[k:n] += wm*(-h[0:nv])
     else:
       kk=-k; nv=n-kk
       grad[kk:n] += wm*(1.0-h[0:nv])
       grad[0:nv] += wm*(-h[kk:n])
  return grad
```
Matches pair orientation? Let's verify with n=2 earlier. p0(w-1),p1(w0),p2(w1). m=0,k=-1: kk1,nv1,kk:n index1 += w*(1-h0); 0:1? grad[0]+= -w*h1. Manual for c_{-1}=h1(1-h0): grad0=-w*h1, grad1=w*(1-h0). Code: grad[1]+=w*(1-h0); grad[0]+=-h1 good. m=2,k=1: nv1; grad[0]+=w*(1-h1); grad[1]+=-w*h0 for c1=h0(1-h1). Good.

Fast formula should match; check.

def run(...):
  t0=...; deadline=t0+budget_s-6
  verify grad
  init h,n
  best...
  rem = deadline - t0
  # quick parent LBFGS if n <=1200
  alloc = min(120, deadline-time-400) # leave time
  if alloc>20: lbfgs parent
  # stochastic pair parent
  alloc = min(120, deadline-time-300)
  if alloc>20: pair parent
  # SLSQP parent maybe 40
  if n<=400 and time<deadline-200: slsqp 40
  # Try nearby resolutions
  near_ns = unique_even([150,200,240,360,420, 2*n? if <=600, 600,750,900]) filter 80<=n<=1200, n != best_n? maybe if >? Sort by closeness to 300/high?
  for ntry in near_ns:
     rem=deadline-time
     if rem<80: break
     # choose alloc based on rem and ntry
     if rem >400: lb_alloc=100 if ntry<=400 else 150 if ntry<=700 else 120; pair_alloc= min(60, rem-lb_alloc-100)
     else: lb_alloc=40, pair_alloc=20
     if lb_alloc<20 and pair_alloc<10: break
     interp h; maybe if c_start > best_c + 0.02 skip? but for nearby maybe.
     lbfgs; pair; update
  # final polish best
  if time<deadline-15: lbfgs best 30
  if time<deadline-5: pair best 20
  finalize
```

Detailed code for pair functions:
```
def pair_apply_single(c, h, hrev, q, eps, n):
    if eps == 0.0:
        return
    dx = 2.0 / n
    coef = eps * dx
    c[n-1-q:2*n-1-q] += coef * (1.0 - h)
    c[q:n+q] += -coef * hrev
```
But for second slice, if we use same hrev view length n, `hrev` length n. Adding `-coef * hrev` to length n slice works. It adds -coef*h[n-1],..., -coef*h[0], correct.
Cross:
```
def pair_apply_c(c,h,hrev,i,j,delta,n):
   s = i-j
   pair_apply_single(c,h,hrev,i,delta,n)
   pair_apply_single(c,h,hrev,j,-delta,n)
   if delta != 0:
      d2 = delta*delta*(2.0/n)
      c[n-1] -= 2.0*d2
      si=s if s>0 else -s
      if si>0:
         c[n-1+si] += d2
         c[n-1-si] += d2
```
But `pair_apply_single` uses h old. If we pass same h for both, okay. Note if `i` or `j` and q order, hrev same. Good.
`pair_trial_max`:
```
def pair_trial_max(c,h,hrev,i,j,delta,n):
   if delta == 0: return float(c.max())
   ct = c.copy()
   pair_apply_c(ct,h,hrev,i,j,delta,n)
   return float(ct.max())
```

def pair_line_search(h,hrev,c,i,j,current_max,dirsign):
   if dirsign >0: maxd = min(1.0-h[i], h[j])
   else: maxd = min(h[i], 1.0-h[j])
   if maxd <= 1e-14: return 0.0, current_max

Candidate moves with random: Need `rng` passed.
```
def candidate_moves(n,g,max_cand,rng):
    moves=[]
    if n <= 350:
       val = g[None,:] - g[:,None]
       np.fill_diagonal(val, -np.inf)
       flat=val.ravel()
       # top positive
       if max_cand >= flat.size:
          cand_indices = np.flatnonzero(flat > 0.0)
       else:
          part = np.argpartition(flat, -max_cand)[-max_cand:]
          # sort top part descending
          part = part[np.argsort(flat[part])[::-1]]
          cand_indices=part
       for idr in cand_indices:
          i = idr // n; j = idr % n
          # filter if derivative positive and feasible possible
          if g[j] <= g[i]: continue
          if g[j]-g[i] < 1e-12: continue
          moves.append((int(i),int(j)))
    else:
       nsamp = min(200000, max(10000, max_cand*20))
       aa = rng.randint(0,n,nsamp); bb=...
       mask = (aa != bb)
       aa=aa[mask]; bb=bb[mask]
       values = g[bb] - g[aa]
       pos = np.where(values > 1e-12)[0]
       if pos.size <= max_cand:
          sel=pos
       else:
          sel = np.argpartition(values[pos], -max_cand)[-max_cand:]
          sel = sel[np.argsort(values[pos][sel])[::-1]]
          sel = pos[sel]
       for idx in sel[:max_cand]:
          i=int(aa[idx]); j=int(bb[idx]); moves.append((i,j))
    # optionally shuffle deterministic? preserve top.
    return moves
```
For n small, `val = g[None,:] - g[:,None]` memory. For n=350 122k; fine. For n=400 160k; okay. If n=600 and max_cand maybe 1000, random.
Need if `max_cand` > positive count; okay.

`stoch_pair_search` loop:

def stoch_pair_search(h,n,deadline,alpha=500.0):
    h = project_feasible(h,n)
    c=c5_full(h,n); current=float(c.max()); best_c=current; best_h=h.copy(); hrev=h[::-1]
    rng=np.random.RandomState(12345)
    alphas=(alpha, 2*alpha, 5*alpha, 10*alpha, 20*alpha)
    alpha_idx=0
    g=None; p=None; moves=[]; move_idx=0; accepts_since=0; no_improve=0; max_cand_n = 200 if n<=350 else 500
    while time.time() < deadline:
       if move_idx >= len(moves) or g is None:
          c=c5_full(h,n); current=float(c.max())
          if current < best_c - 1e-13: best_c=current; best_h=h.copy()
          p = smooth_weights(c, alphas[alpha_idx])
          g = grad_weighted(h,n,p)
          moves = candidate_moves(n,g,max_cand_n,rng)
          move_idx=0
          accepts_since=0
          if not moves:
             # random moves
             moves = [(rng.randint(0,n), rng.randint(0,n)) for _ in range(200)] # no nested? list comprehension okay? It's not lambda/closure? Comprehension okay? "no closures or lambdas" maybe comprehensions? They are syntax not closures? To be safe avoid? Comprehensions create function internally? Usually no explicit closure? But might be considered? Better use loop.
          no_improve=0
       if move_idx >= len(moves):
          alpha_idx = min(alpha_idx+1, len(alphas)-1)
          continue
       i,j=moves[move_idx]; move_idx+=1
       # choose dir based on g
       if g[j] > g[i]: dirsign=1
       else: dirsign=-1
       d,best_val = pair_line_search(h,hrev,c,i,j,current,dirsign)
       if d == 0 and (rng.rand() < 0.05): # try opposite random
          d,best_val = pair_line_search(h,hrev,c,i,j,current,-dirsign)
       if d != 0 and best_val < current - 1e-14:
          pair_apply_c(c,h,hrev,i,j,d,n)
          current = best_val
          hrev = h[::-1]
          accepts_since += 1
          if current < best_c - 1e-13:
             best_c=current; best_h=h.copy()
          if accepts_since >= 15:
             # recompute c to avoid drift
             c = c5_full(h,n); current=float(c.max())
             if current < best_c -1e-13: update
             accepts_since=0
             # refresh gradient now
             move_idx = len(moves)+1 # trigger
       else:
          no_improve += 1
          if no_improve > len(moves)*1.2? and move_idx<len? We can if all candidates tried no improve: alpha_idx++.
```

Now, `smooth_weights(c, alpha)`:
```
def smooth_weights(c,alpha):
   if alpha <= 1e-8:
      return np.full_like(c,1.0/len(c))
   mx = c.max()
   sh = alpha*(c-mx)
   # sh max 0, may have very negative; exp underflow okay
   w = np.exp(sh)
   s=w.sum()
   if s <= 0:
      idx=np.argmax(c); w.fill(0); w[idx]=1
   else: w/=s
   return w
```
Since c max included, max sh=0; no need mx shift.

`smooth_objective` uses logsumexp with cmax:
```
def smooth_objective(x,n,alpha,mu):
   dx=2/n; c=...; cmax=c.max();
   if alpha <= 1e-8: val = cmax + mu*(sum-target)^2
   else:
      sh=alpha*(c-cmax); mx=np.max(sh)  # 0
      if mx > 500? not; sh<=0. logsum = mx + log(sum(exp(sh-mx)))
      val = cmax + logsum/alpha
   val += mu*(x.sum()-n/2)**2
   return float(val)
```
Since sh max 0, logsum = log(sum(exp(sh))). If alpha large, exp negative underflow but sum at least 1. Good.
But derivative of logsumexp(alpha c) = exp(at c)/sum * alpha / alpha? = p grad c. Our val = cmax + logsum/alpha where logsum = log sum exp(alpha(c-cmax)) = logsumexp(alpha c) - alpha cmax. derivative p grad c. Good.
`mu` maybe.

if mu: g += 2*mu*s*ones
return g
```

def lbfgs_refine(h,n,deadline):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c5_max(h,n)
    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem < 5: return best_h,best_c
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    configs=[(20.0,1e-3,1.0),(80.0,1e-3,1.0),(300.0,1e-2,0.8),(1000.0,3e-2,0.7),(3000.0,1e-1,0.6),(10000.0,3e-1,0.5),(30000.0,1.0,0.4)]
    if n > 350: ... reduce factors
    for alpha,mu,factor in configs:
       rem=deadline-time.time()
       if rem < 3: break
       base = 200000.0 / n
       maxit = int(max(10, min(200, base*factor)))
       # also limit by rem: maybe maxit=min(maxit, int(rem*20+5))
       try:
          res=minimize(smooth_objective,h,jac=smooth_gradient,args=(n,alpha,mu),method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,options={'maxiter':maxit,'ftol':1e-18,'gtol':1e-14,'maxfun':maxit*20,'maxls':20})
       except Exception: break
       hnew=project_feasible(res.x,n)
       cnew=c5_max(hnew,n)
       if cnew < best_c - 1e-15:
          best_c=cnew; best_h=hnew.copy()
       h=best_h  # continue from best
    return best_h,best_c
```
Base 200000/n: n300 =>666, cap200; n600=>333 cap200; n900=>222 cap200. Factor lowers. Sum maxit maybe 200+160... 1000. Might be too many but okay? Need conservative. Use base 50000/n: n300 166; n600 83; n900 55. Sum factors ~5 -> 400/400/275. Good. Options.

```

def make_fallback_h(n,seed):
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n
   best=None; bestc=1e9
   # constant
   candidates=[]
   candidates.append(np.full(n,0.5))
   for amp in (0.2,0.35,0.45):
      candidates.append(0.5+amp*np.sin(np.pi*x))
      candidates.append(0.5+amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*x))
   # random smooth
   for k in range(3):
      # use low freq 1..4 amplitudes random
      h=np.full(n,0.5)
      for m in range(1,5):
         h += rng.normal(0,0.08)*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x/2.0 + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
      candidates.append(h)
   for cand in candidates:
      hp=project_feasible(cand,n)
      cc=c5_max(hp,n)
      if cc<bestc: bestc=cc; best=hp
   return best
```
No nested. Good. If n large, c5_max for 10 candidates O(10 n^2) maybe okay n=600 7M, fine.

Now, `pair_line_search` performance: It calls `pair_trial_max` which copies c and does slice operations. For each step, pair_apply_single uses `coef*(1.0-h)` and `-coef*hrev` which creates temporary arrays length n each. Two per trial -> 2n allocations. Could be expensive. We can optimize by precomputing `one_minus_h` and using? h changes on accepts; line search uses same h. In `pair_line_search`, we can create `omh = 1.0 - h` and pass. But pair_trial_max top-level would accept omh/hrev. Let's modify to reduce allocations:
`pair_apply_single(c, omh, hrev, q, coef, n)`: c slice += coef*omh; c slice += -coef*hrev. Here coef=eps*dx. We can compute `coef` for each q. For trial, call with `eps=delta` and `-delta`. It still creates `coef*omh` temporary but `omh` stored. Could further precompute `v1 = 1-h`? temporary for multiplication needed. We can do `c[slice1] += coef * omh` creates temp length n. Same for hrev. Original also temp. Slight save precomputing omh once per line search. Good.
Define:
```
def pair_add_single(c, omh, hrev, q, coef, n):
   if coef == 0: return
   c[n-1-q:2*n-1-q] += coef * omh
   c[q:n+q] += -coef * hrev
```

pair_add_single(ct,omh,hrev,i,coef1,n); pair_add_single(...j,coef2)
...
```

def pair_trial_max(c, omh, hrev, i,j,delta,n): ...
def pair_line_search(...): omh=1.0-h; ... call pair_trial_max(c,omh,hrev,...)
```

def pair_apply_move(h,c,omh,hrev,i,j,delta,n):

def c5_full(h, n):
    if n <= 0: return np.zeros(0)

`project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(x, n):
    if n <= 0: return np.array([], dtype=float)
    try: x = np.array(x, dtype=float, copy=True)
    except Exception: return np.full(n, 0.5)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return np.full(n,0.5)
    if x.size != n:
       # interpolate? If size mismatch, resize
       if x.size ==0: return np.full(n,0.5)
       old=n? assume.
    x = np.clip(x,0.0,1.0)
    target = n/2.0
    s = float(np.sum(x))
    if abs(s - target) < 1e-12:
        return x
    lo=-1.0; hi=1.0
    # If target greater than n? impossible; if target<0 no.
    for _ in range(70):
       mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
       psum = float(np.sum(np.clip(x - mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
       if psum > target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    lam=(lo+hi)*0.5
    p=np.clip(x-lam,0.0,1.0)
    diff = target - float(np.sum(p))
    # adjust few times
    for _ in range(5):
       if abs(diff) < 1e-14: break
       if diff > 0:
          idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-p))
          room=1.0-p[idx]
          if room <= 1e-15: break
          add = min(diff, room)
          p[idx] = p[idx] + add
       else:
          idx=int(np.argmin(p))
          room=p[idx]
          if room <= 1e-15: break
          add = max(diff, -room)
          p[idx] = p[idx] + add
       diff = target - float(np.sum(p))
    return p
```
If x size != n, resizing? In our code x size matches. Could add:
```
if x.size != n:
   old = x.size
   xs = (np.arange(old)+0.5)*2/old if old>0 else np.zeros(0)
   xt = (np.arange(n)+0.5)*2/n
   x = np.interp(xt, xs, x) if old>0 else np.full(n,0.5)
```
Good.

```
def check_gradient_formula():
    n=16
    rng=np.random.RandomState(0)
    h=np.clip(rng.rand(n),0.0,1.0)
    h=sum proj? not needed.
    c=c5_full(h,n)
    p=np.random.rand(c.size); p=np.exp(p-p.max()); p/=p.sum()
    g=grad_weighted_fast(h,n,p)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)): return False
    eps=1e-6
    for idx in (0,3,8,15):
       h2=h.copy(); h2[idx]+=eps
       f1=float(np.dot(p,c5_full(h2,n)))
       h2[idx]-=2*eps
       f0=float(np.dot(p,c5_full(h2,n)))
       fd=(f1-f0)/(2*eps)
       if abs(fd-g[idx]) > 1e-4*(1+abs(fd)):
          return False
    return True
```

`stoch_pair_search` code in detail:
```
def stoch_pair_search(h, n, deadline, alpha=500.0):
    if n <=1: return h, c5_max(h,n)
    h = project_feasible(h,n)
    c = c5_full(h,n); current = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    best_h = h.copy(); best_c = current
    rng = np.random.RandomState(987654)
    alphas = (alpha, 4*alpha, 16*alpha, 64*alpha, 256*alpha)
    aidx=0
    g=None; moves=[]; move_idx=0; accepts=0; improved_cycle=False
    omh = 1.0 - h; hrev = h[::-1]
    if n <= 250: max_cand = 1200
    elif n <= 450: max_cand = 800
    elif n <= 700: max_cand = 500
    else: max_cand = 300
    while time.time() < deadline:
       if move_idx >= len(moves):
          # recompute current to ensure truth
          c = c5_full(h,n); current = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
          if current < best_c:
              best_c = current; best_h = h.copy()
          elif current > best_c + 1e-8:
              h = best_h.copy(); c = c5_full(h,n); current = float(np.max(c))
          p = smooth_weights(c, alphas[aidx])
          g = grad_weighted(h,n,p)
          if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
              g = np.zeros(n)
          moves = candidate_moves(n,g,max_cand,rng)
          if not moves:
              moves = []
              for _ in range(min(500, 20*n)):
                  i = int(rng.randint(0,n)); j = int(rng.randint(0,n))
                  if i != j: moves.append((i,j))
          move_idx=0; accepts=0; improved_cycle=False
          if not moves: break
       if move_idx >= len(moves):
          if not improved_cycle and aidx < len(alphas)-1: aidx +=1
          continue
       i,j = moves[move_idx]; move_idx += 1
       if g[j] > g[i] + 1e-14:
           dirsign=1
       else:
           dirsign=-1
       d, val = pair_line_search(h, omh, hrev, c, i, j, current, dirsign)
       if (d == 0.0 or val >= current - 1e-14) and (rng.rand() < 0.1 or d == 0.0):
           d2, val2 = pair_line_search(h, omh, hrev, c, i, j, current, -dirsign)
           if d2 != 0.0 and val2 < current - 1e-14:
               d=d2; val=val2
       if d != 0.0 and val < current - 1e-14:
           pair_apply_move(h,c,omh,hrev,i,j,d,n)
           current = val
           omh = 1.0 - h; hrev = h[::-1]
           improved_cycle = True
           if current < best_c:
               best_c = current; best_h = h.copy()
           accepts += 1
           if accepts >= 25:
               c = c5_full(h,n); current = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
               if current < best_c:
                  best_c = current; best_h = h.copy()
               elif current > best_c + 1e-8:
                  h = best_h.copy(); c = c5_full(h,n); current = float(np.max(c))
               omh = 1.0 - h; hrev = h[::-1]
               accepts = 0
    # final recompute best
    c_best = c5_full(best_h,n); c_best_val = float(np.max(c_best)) if c_best.size else 0.0
    # also compute current h in case drift and better? We can check h too
    c_cur = c5_full(h,n); c_cur_val = float(np.max(c_cur)) if c_cur.size else 0.0
    if c_cur_val < c_best_val:
        best_h = h.copy(); c_best_val = c_cur_val
    return best_h, c_best_val
```

   limit = min(deadline - 260, time.time() + 50)
   hS,cS = slsqp_refine(best_h,best_n, limit, 5000.0, 60)
   evaluate...

def perturb_h(rng,h,sigma):
   n=len(h)
   if n==0: return h.copy()
   h2 = h + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
   return project_feasible(h2,n)

```
def candidate_moves(n,g,h,max_cand,rng):
    moves=[]
    if n <= 1: return moves
    if n <= 350:
       val = g[None, :] - g[:, None]
       np.fill_diagonal(val, -np.inf)
       flat = val.ravel()
       pos_count = int(np.count_nonzero(flat > 1e-12))
       if pos_count <= 0:
           return moves
       if pos_count <= max_cand:
           indices = np.flatnonzero(flat > 1e-12)
       else:
           indices = np.argpartition(flat, -max_cand)[-max_cand:]
           indices = indices[np.argsort(flat[indices])[::-1]]
       for idx in indices:
           i = int(idx // n); j = int(idx % n)
           possible = (h[i] < 1.0 - 1e-14 and h[j] > 1e-14) or (h[i] > 1e-14 and h[j] < 1.0 - 1e-14)
           if possible:
               moves.append((i,j))
    else:
       nsamp = int(min(200000, max(10000, max_cand * 20)))
       aa = rng.randint(0,n,nsamp)
       bb = rng.randint(0,n,nsamp)
       mask = aa != bb
       aa = aa[mask]; bb = bb[mask]
       values = g[bb] - g[aa]
       pos = values > 1e-12
       pidx = np.flatnonzero(pos)
       if pidx.size == 0: return moves
       if pidx.size <= max_cand:
          sel = pidx
       else:
          sub = values[pidx]
          selrel = np.argpartition(sub, -max_cand)[-max_cand:]
          selrel = selrel[np.argsort(sub[selrel])[::-1]]
          sel = pidx[selrel]
       for idx in sel:
          i=int(aa[idx]); j=int(bb[idx])
          possible = ...
          if possible: moves.append((i,j))
    # Maybe shuffle? top order deterministic. Could random shuffle to avoid same pairs causing no improve? Not needed.
    return moves
```
For n small, if pos_count > max_cand, top max by absolute gradient diff. Good.

In random sampling, if n large and max_cand 300, nsamp 6000. okay.

def pair_line_search(h, omh, hrev, c, i, j, current_max, dirsign):
   n = len(h)
   if dirsign > 0:
      maxd = min(1.0 - h[i], h[j])
   else:
      maxd = min(h[i], 1.0 - h[j])
   if maxd <= 1e-15: return 0.0, current_max
   best_val = current_max; best_d=0.0
   dx=2.0/n
   # fractions
   for f in _FRACS:
      d = dirsign * f * maxd
      if feasible...
      val = pair_trial_max(...)
      if val < best_val - 1e-14: best_val=val; best_d=d
   for cap in _CAPS:
      if cap < maxd: d=dirsign*cap
      else: d=dirsign*maxd
      ...
   if best_d != 0.0:
      for mult in _MULTS:
         d = best_d * mult
         clip
         val=...
   return best_d,best_val
```
Need make `pair_trial_max` call with `omh,hrev` and `n`. To reduce repeated feasible code, create helper top-level `valid_step(d, dirsign, maxd)`? It would be small but no closure. We can implement top-level:
```
def clip_step(d, dirsign, maxd):
    if dirsign > 0:
       if d <= 1e-15: return 0.0
       if d > maxd: d = maxd
       if d <= 0: return 0.0
    else:
       if d >= -1e-15: return 0.0
       if d < -maxd: d = -maxd
       if d >= 0: return 0.0
    return d
```
Then line search uses. Good.

`pair_trial_max` returns float. If `d` negative, delta negative. Good.

Potential duplicate evaluations: If `d` 0, skip.

```python
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

_FAST_GRAD = True

_FRACS = (
    1.0, 0.6, 0.4, 0.25, 0.15, 0.08, 0.04,
    0.02, 0.01, 0.005, 0.002, 0.001, 0.0004, 0.0001, 0.00003
)
_CAPS = (0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005, 0.002, 0.001, 0.0004, 0.0001)
_MULTS = (0.5, 0.8, 1.25, 0.7, 1.3, 0.9, 1.1)


def c5_full(h, n):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=float)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * (2.0 / float(n))


def c5_max(h, n):
    c = c5_full(h, n)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.max(c))


def project_feasible(x, n):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    try:
        x = np.array(x, dtype=float, copy=True)
    except Exception:
        return np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float)

    if x.size != n:
        old = x.size
        if old == 0:
            x = np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float)
        else:
            xs = (np.arange(old) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(old))
            xt = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
            x = np.interp(xt, xs, x)

    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    s = float(np.sum(x))
    if abs(s - target) < 1e-12:
        return x

    lo = -1.0
    hi = 1.0
    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        psum = float(np.sum(np.clip(x - mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if psum > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid

    lam = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    p = np.clip(x - lam, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(np.sum(p))

    for _ in range(6):
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - p))
            room = 1.0 - p[idx]
            if room <= 1e-15:
                break
            add = min(diff, room)
            p[idx] = p[idx] + add
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmin(p))
            room = p[idx]
            if room <= 1e-15:
                break
            add = max(diff, -room)
            p[idx] = p[idx] + add
        diff = target - float(np.sum(p))
    return p


def interp_to_n(h, nold, nnew):
    nold = int(nold)
    nnew = int(nnew)
    if nnew <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if nold == nnew:
        return project_feasible(h, nnew)
    if nold <= 0:
        return project_feasible(np.full(nnew, 0.5), nnew)

    xs = (np.arange(nold) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(nold))
    xt = (np.arange(nnew) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(nnew))
    hnew = np.interp(xt, xs, h)
    return project_feasible(hnew, nnew)


def smooth_weights(c, alpha):
    m = c.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=float)
    if alpha <= 1e-8:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=float)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    sh = alpha * (c - cmax)
    w = np.exp(sh)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        w.fill(0.0)
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        w[idx] = 1.0
    else:
        w /= s
    return w


def grad_weighted_slow(h, n, p):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for m in range(L):
        wm = p[m] * dx
        if wm < 1e-300:
            continue
        k = m - (n - 1)
        if k >= 0:
            nv = n - k
            grad[0:nv] += wm * (1.0 - h[k:n])
            grad[k:n] += wm * (-h[0:nv])
        else:
            kk = -k
            nv = n - kk
            grad[kk:n] += wm * (1.0 - h[0:nv])
            grad[0:nv] += wm * (-h[kk:n])
    return grad


def grad_weighted_fast(h, n, p):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    W = p * dx
    L = 2 * n - 1

    cumW = np.empty(L + 1, dtype=float)
    cumW[0] = 0.0
    np.cumsum(W, out=cumW[1:])

    idx = np.arange(n)
    starts = (n - 1) - idx
    ends = (2 * n - 1) - idx
    A = cumW[ends] - cumW[starts]

    Bm = np.convolve(h, W, mode="full")[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    Bp = np.convolve(h, W[::-1], mode="full")[n - 1:2 * n - 1]

    return A - Bp - Bm


def grad_weighted(h, n, p):
    if _FAST_GRAD:
        g = grad_weighted_fast(h, n, p)
        if np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            return g
    return grad_weighted_slow(h, n, p)


def check_gradient_formula():
    if not _FAST_GRAD:
        return False
    n = 16
    rng = np.random.RandomState(123)
    h = np.clip(rng.rand(n), 0.0, 1.0)
    c = c5_full(h, n)
    p = np.random.rand(c.size)
    p = np.exp(p - np.max(p))
    p /= np.sum(p)

    g = grad_weighted_fast(h, n, p)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        return False

    eps = 1e-6
    for ii in (0, 3, 8, 15):
        h2 = h.copy()
        h2[ii] += eps
        f1 = float(np.dot(p, c5_full(h2, n)))
        h2[ii] -= 2.0 * eps
        f0 = float(np.dot(p, c5_full(h2, n)))
        fd = (f1 - f0) / (2.0 * eps)
        if abs(fd - g[ii]) > 1e-4 * (1.0 + abs(fd)):
            return False
    return True


def smooth_objective(x, n, alpha, mu):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full") * dx
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if alpha <= 1e-8:
        val = cmax
    else:
        sh = alpha * (c - cmax)
        logsum = float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh))))
        val = cmax + logsum / alpha
    s = float(np.sum(x)) - 0.5 * float(n)
    val += mu * s * s
    return float(val)


def smooth_gradient(x, n, alpha, mu):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full") * dx
    p = smooth_weights(c, alpha)
    g = grad_weighted(x, n, p)
    if mu != 0.0:
        s = float(np.sum(x)) - 0.5 * float(n)
        g += (2.0 * mu * s) * np.ones(n)
    return g


def slsqp_objective(x, n, alpha):
    return smooth_objective(x, n, alpha, 0.0)


def slsqp_gradient(x, n, alpha):
    return smooth_gradient(x, n, alpha, 0.0)


def sum_eq(x, n):
    return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * float(n))


def sum_eq_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones(len(x), dtype=float)


def lbfgs_refine(h, n, deadline):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5_max(best_h, n)

    if time.time() >= deadline - 3.0:
        return best_h, best_c

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    configs = (
        (20.0, 1e-3, 1.00),
        (80.0, 1e-3, 1.00),
        (300.0, 1e-2, 0.85),
        (1000.0, 3e-2, 0.75),
        (3000.0, 1e-1, 0.60),
        (10000.0, 3e-1, 0.50),
        (30000.0, 1.0, 0.40),
        (100000.0, 3.0, 0.30),
    )

    for cfg in configs:
        alpha, mu, factor = cfg
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 4.0:
            break

        base = 35000.0 / float(max(1, n))
        maxit = int(base * factor)
        maxit = max(10, min(160, maxit))
        maxit = max(5, min(maxit, int(rem * 12) + 10))

        try:
            res = minimize(
                smooth_objective,
                h,
                jac=smooth_gradient,
                args=(n, alpha, mu),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": maxit,
                    "ftol": 1e-16,
                    "gtol": 1e-13,
                    "maxfun": max(20, 20 * maxit),
                    "maxls": 20,
                },
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if res is None or res.x is None:
            break
        hnew = project_feasible(res.x, n)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(hnew)):
            break
        cnew = c5_max(hnew, n)
        if cnew < best_c - 1e-15:
            best_c = cnew
            best_h = hnew.copy()
        h = best_h

    return best_h, best_c


def slsqp_refine(h, n, deadline, alpha=5000.0, maxiter=80):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5_max(best_h, n)

    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem < 8.0:
        return best_h, best_c

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{"type": "eq", "fun": sum_eq, "jac": sum_eq_jac, "args": (n,)}]
    maxit = int(min(maxiter, max(8, int(rem * 2) + 8)))

    try:
        res = minimize(
            slsqp_objective,
            h,
            jac=slsqp_gradient,
            args=(n, alpha),
            method="SLSQP",
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            options={"maxiter": maxit, "ftol": 1e-16},
        )
    except Exception:
        return best_h, best_c

    if res is not None and res.x is not None:
        hnew = project_feasible(res.x, n)
        if np.all(np.isfinite(hnew)):
            cnew = c5_max(hnew, n)
            if cnew < best_c - 1e-15:
                best_c = cnew
                best_h = hnew.copy()

    return best_h, best_c


def clip_step(d, dirsign, maxd):
    if dirsign > 0:
        if d <= 1e-15:
            return 0.0
        if d > maxd:
            d = maxd
        if d <= 0.0:
            return 0.0
    else:
        if d >= -1e-15:
            return 0.0
        if d < -maxd:
            d = -maxd
        if d >= 0.0:
            return 0.0
    return float(d)


def pair_add_single(c, omh, hrev, q, coef, n):
    if coef == 0.0:
        return
    c[n - 1 - q:2 * n - 1 - q] += coef * omh
    c[q:n + q] += -coef * hrev


def pair_trial_max(c, omh, hrev, i, j, delta, n):
    if delta == 0.0:
        return float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    ct = c.copy()
    pair_add_single(ct, omh, hrev, i, delta * dx, n)
    pair_add_single(ct, omh, hrev, j, -delta * dx, n)

    dd = delta * delta * dx
    if dd != 0.0:
        s = i - j
        if s < 0:
            s = -s
        ct[n - 1] -= 2.0 * dd
        if s > 0:
            ct[n - 1 + s] += dd
            ct[n - 1 - s] += dd
    return float(np.max(ct)) if ct.size else 0.0


def pair_apply_move(h, c, omh, hrev, i, j, delta, n):
    if delta == 0.0:
        return
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    pair_add_single(c, omh, hrev, i, delta * dx, n)
    pair_add_single(c, omh, hrev, j, -delta * dx, n)

    dd = delta * delta * dx
    if dd != 0.0:
        s = i - j
        if s < 0:
            s = -s
        c[n - 1] -= 2.0 * dd
        if s > 0:
            c[n - 1 + s] += dd
            c[n - 1 - s] += dd

    h[i] += delta
    h[j] -= delta


def pair_line_search(h, omh, hrev, c, i, j, current_max, dirsign):
    n = len(h)
    if dirsign > 0:
        maxd = min(1.0 - h[i], h[j])
    else:
        maxd = min(h[i], 1.0 - h[j])

    if maxd <= 1e-15:
        return 0.0, current_max

    best_val = current_max
    best_d = 0.0

    for f in _FRACS:
        d = clip_step(dirsign * f * maxd, dirsign, maxd)
        if d == 0.0:
            continue
        val = pair_trial_max(c, omh, hrev, i, j, d, n)
        if val < best_val - 1e-14:
            best_val = val
            best_d = d

    for cap in _CAPS:
        d = clip_step(dirsign * cap, dirsign, maxd)
        if d == 0.0:
            continue
        val = pair_trial_max(c, omh, hrev, i, j, d, n)
        if val < best_val - 1e-14:
            best_val = val
            best_d = d

    if best_d != 0.0:
        for mult in _MULTS:
            d = clip_step(best_d * mult, dirsign, maxd)
            if d == 0.0:
                continue
            val = pair_trial_max(c, omh, hrev, i, j, d, n)
            if val < best_val - 1e-14:
                best_val = val
                best_d = d

    return best_d, best_val


def candidate_moves(n, g, h, max_cand, rng):
    moves = []
    if n <= 1:
        return moves

    def feasible_pair_ii(i, j):
        return (h[i] < 1.0 - 1e-14 and h[j] > 1e-14) or \
               (h[i] > 1e-14 and h[j] < 1.0 - 1e-14)

    if n <= 350:
        val = g[None, :] - g[:, None]
        np.fill_diagonal(val, -np.inf)
        flat = val.ravel()
        pos_count = int(np.count_nonzero(flat > 1e-12))
        if pos_count <= 0:
            return moves

        if pos_count <= max_cand:
            indices = np.flatnonzero(flat > 1e-12)
        else:
            indices = np.argpartition(flat, -max_cand)[-max_cand:]
            indices = indices[np.argsort(flat[indices])[::-1]]

        for idx in indices:
            i = int(idx // n)
            j = int(idx % n)
            if feasible_pair_ii(i, j):
                moves.append((i, j))
    else:
        nsamp = int(min(200000, max(10000, max_cand * 20)))
        aa = rng.randint(0, n, nsamp)
        bb = rng.randint(0, n, nsamp)
        mask = aa != bb
        aa = aa[mask]
        bb = bb[mask]
        if aa.size == 0:
            return moves

        values = g[bb] - g[aa]
        pos = values > 1e-12
        pidx = np.flatnonzero(pos)
        if pidx.size == 0:
            return moves

        if pidx.size <= max_cand:
            sel = pidx
        else:
            sub = values[pidx]
            selrel = np.argpartition(sub, -max_cand)[-max_cand:]
            selrel = selrel[np.argsort(sub[selrel])[::-1]]
            sel = pidx[selrel]

        for idx in sel:
            i = int(aa[idx])
            j = int(bb[idx])
            if feasible_pair_ii(i, j):
                moves.append((i, j))

    return moves


def stoch_pair_search(h, n, deadline, alpha=500.0):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = c5_full(h, n)
    current = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = current

    if n <= 1:
        return best_h, best_c

    rng = np.random.RandomState(987654321)
    alphas = (float(alpha), 4.0 * float(alpha), 16.0 * float(alpha),
              64.0 * float(alpha), 256.0 * float(alpha))
    aidx = 0

    g = None
    moves = []
    move_idx = 0
    accepts = 0
    improved_cycle = False

    omh = 1.0 - h
    hrev = h[::-1]

    if n <= 250:
        max_cand = 1200
    elif n <= 450:
        max_cand = 800
    elif n <= 700:
        max_cand = 500
    else:
        max_cand = 300

    while time.time() < deadline:
        if move_idx >= len(moves):
            c = c5_full(h, n)
            current = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
            if current < best_c - 1e-13:
                best_c = current
                best_h = h.copy()
            elif current > best_c + 1e-8:
                h = best_h.copy()
                c = c5_full(h, n)
                current = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0

            p = smooth_weights(c, alphas[aidx])
            g = grad_weighted(h, n, p)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
                g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

            moves = candidate_moves(n, g, h, max_cand, rng)
            if not moves:
                moves = []
                for _ in range(min(500, 20 * n)):
                    i = int(rng.randint(0, n))
                    j = int(rng.randint(0, n))
                    if i != j:
                        moves.append((i, j))
            move_idx = 0
            accepts = 0
            improved_cycle = False
            if not moves:
                break

        if move_idx >= len(moves):
            if not improved_cycle and aidx < len(alphas) - 1:
                aidx += 1
            continue

        i, j = moves[move_idx]
        move_idx += 1

        if g[j] > g[i] + 1e-14:
            dirsign = 1
        else:
            dirsign = -1

        d1, v1 = pair_line_search(h, omh, hrev, c, i, j, current, dirsign)
        d2 = 0.0
        v2 = current

        if d1 == 0.0 or rng.rand() < 0.10:
            d2, v2 = pair_line_search(h, omh, hrev, c, i, j, current, -dirsign)

        d = 0.0
        val = current
        if d1 != 0.0 and v1 < current - 1e-14:
            d = d1
            val = v1
        if d2 != 0.0 and v2 < current - 1e-14:
            if d == 0.0 or v2 < val:
                d = d2
                val = v2

        if d != 0.0 and val < current - 1e-14:
            old_current = current
            pair_apply_move(h, c, omh, hrev, i, j, d, n)
            new_current = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0

            if new_current < old_current - 1e-14:
                current = new_current
                omh = 1.0 - h
                hrev = h[::-1]
                improved_cycle = True
                accepts += 1
                if current < best_c - 1e-14:
                    best_c = current
                    best_h = h.copy()

                if accepts >= 25:
                    c = c5_full(h, n)
                    current = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
                    omh = 1.0 - h
                    hrev = h[::-1]
                    accepts = 0
                    if current < best_c - 1e-14:
                        best_c = current
                        best_h = h.copy()
                    elif current > best_c + 1e-8:
                        h = best_h.copy()
                        c = c5_full(h, n)
                        current = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
                        omh = 1.0 - h
                        hrev = h[::-1]
            else:
                h = best_h.copy()
                c = c5_full(h, n)
                current = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
                omh = 1.0 - h
                hrev = h[::-1]
                if current < best_c - 1e-14:
                    best_c = current
                    best_h = h.copy()

    cbest = c5_full(best_h, n)
    cbest_val = float(np.max(cbest)) if cbest.size else 0.0

    ccur = c5_full(h, n)
    ccur_val = float(np.max(ccur)) if ccur.size else 0.0
    if ccur_val < cbest_val - 1e-14:
        best_h = h.copy()
        cbest_val = ccur_val

    return best_h, cbest_val


def perturb_h(rng, h, sigma):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return h.copy()
    h2 = h + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
    return project_feasible(h2, n)


def make_fallback_h(n, seed):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))

    best = project_feasible(np.full(n, 0.5), n)
    best_c = c5_max(best, n)

    for amp in (0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.4):
        h = 0.5 + amp * np.sin(np.pi * x)
        hp = project_feasible(h, n)
        cc = c5_max(hp, n)
        if cc < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c = cc
            best = hp

        h = 0.5 + amp * np.sin(np.pi * x + 0.7)
        hp = project_feasible(h, n)
        cc = c5_max(hp, n)
        if cc < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c = cc
            best = hp

    for _ in range(5):
        h = np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float)
        for m in range(1, 6):
            amp = rng.normal(0.0, 0.08)
            ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
            h += amp * np.sin((np.pi * m * x) + ph)
        hp = project_feasible(h, n)
        cc = c5_max(hp, n)
        if cc < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c = cc
            best = hp

    return best


def evaluate_if_better(best_h, best_c, best_n, cand_h, cand_n):
    if cand_h is None:
        return best_h, best_c, best_n
    cand_h = np.asarray(cand_h, dtype=float)
    cand_n = int(cand_n)
    if cand_n != len(cand_h):
        cand_n = len(cand_h)
    if cand_n <= 0:
        return best_h, best_c, best_n

    c = c5_max(cand_h, cand_n)
    if not np.isfinite(c):
        return best_h, best_c, best_n
    if c < best_c - 1e-12:
        return cand_h.copy(), c, cand_n
    return best_h, best_c, best_n


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    global _FAST_GRAD

    t0 = time.time()
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t0 + budget_s - 10.0

    if _FAST_GRAD and not check_gradient_formula():
        _FAST_GRAD = False

    h0 = None
    n0 = 0
    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n0 = int(len(h0))
    except Exception:
        h0 = None
        n0 = 0

    if h0 is None or n0 < 20:
        n0 = 300
        h0 = make_fallback_h(n0, int(seed))

    best_h = project_feasible(h0, n0)
    best_c = c5_max(best_h, best_n := n0) if False else c5_max(best_h, n0)
    best_n = n0

    if time.time() < deadline - 320.0:
        lim = min(deadline - 320.0, time.time() + 140.0)
        hA, cA = lbfgs_refine(best_h, best_n, lim)
        best_h, best_c, best_n = evaluate_if_better(best_h, best_c, best_n, hA, best_n)

    if time.time() < deadline - 250.0:
        lim = min(deadline - 250.0, time.time() + 120.0)
        hP, cP = stoch_pair_search(best_h, best_n, lim, 500.0)
        best_h, best_c, best_n = evaluate_if_better(best_h, best_c, best_n, hP, best_n)

    if best_n <= 350 and time.time() < deadline - 260.0:
        lim = min(deadline - 260.0, time.time() + 50.0)
        hS, cS = slsqp_refine(best_h, best_n, lim, 5000.0, 60)
        best_h, best_c, best_n = evaluate_if_better(best_h, best_c, best_n, hS, best_n)

    raw_ns = [600, 750, 900, 450, 500, 360, 240, 200, 150]
    for fac in (1.5, 2.0, 2.5, 3.0):
        nt = int(round(n0 * fac / 20.0) * 20)
        if nt % 2 != 0:
            nt += 1
        raw_ns.append(nt)

    seen = set()
    ns = []
    for nt in raw_ns:
        nt = max(80, min(1100, int(nt)))
        if nt % 2 != 0:
            nt += 1
        if nt in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(nt)
        if abs(nt - best_n) > max(20, int(0.05 * best_n)):
            ns.append(nt)

    ns.sort(reverse=True)

    for nt in ns:
        if time.time() >= deadline - 90.0:
            break
        if abs(nt - best_n) <= max(20, int(0.05 * best_n)):
            continue

        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if nt <= 300:
            lb = 35
            pr = 25
        elif nt <= 500:
            lb = 55
            pr = 35
        elif nt <= 800:
            lb = 90
            pr = 55
        else:
            lb = 100
            pr = 45

        if lb + pr > rem - 80.0:
            lb = int(max(20, rem * 0.35))
            pr = int(max(10, rem * 0.20))
        if lb < 20 and pr < 10:
            continue

        h_start = interp_to_n(best_h, best_n, nt)
        h_t = h_start
        if lb > 20:
            h_t, c_t = lbfgs_refine(h_start, nt, time.time() + lb)

        best_h, best_c, best_n = evaluate_if_better(best_h, best_c, best_n, h_t, nt)

        if pr > 10 and time.time() < deadline - 70.0:
            lim = min(deadline - 70.0, time.time() + pr)
            h_p, c_p = stoch_pair_search(h_t, nt, lim, 1000.0)
            best_h, best_c, best_n = evaluate_if_better(best_h, best_c, best_n, h_p, nt)

        if nt <= 350 and time.time() < deadline - 60.0:
            lim = min(deadline - 60.0, time.time() + 25.0)
            h_s, c_s = slsqp_refine(h_t, nt, lim, 5000.0, 40)
            best_h, best_c, best_n = evaluate_if_better(best_h, best_c, best_n, h_s, nt)

    if time.time() < deadline - 150.0:
        rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed) + 12345)
        for sig in (0.005, 0.01, 0.02):
            if time.time() >= deadline - 100.0:
                break
            h_r = perturb_h(rng, best_h, sig)
            c_r = c5_max(h_r, best_n)
            if c_r < best_c + 0.005:
                if time.time() < deadline - 80.0:
                    lim = min(deadline - 80.0, time.time() + 45.0)
                    h_r2, c_r2 = lbfgs_refine(h_r, best_n, lim)
                    best_h, best_c, best_n = evaluate_if_better(best_h, best_c, best_n, h_r2, best_n)

                if time.time() < deadline - 50.0:
                    lim = min(deadline - 50.0, time.time() + 30.0)
                    h_r3, c_r3 = stoch_pair_search(best_h, best_n, lim, 2000.0)
                    best_h, best_c, best_n = evaluate_if_better(best_h, best_c, best_n, h_r3, best_n)

    if time.time() < deadline - 20.0:
        lim = min(deadline - 5.0, time.time() + 25.0)
        hF, cF = lbfgs_refine(best_h, best_n, lim)
        best_h, best_c, best_n = evaluate_if_better(best_h, best_c, best_n, hF, best_n)

    if time.time() < deadline - 5.0:
        hF, cF = stoch_pair_search(best_h, best_n, max(time.time(), deadline - 1.0), 5000.0)
        best_h, best_c, best_n = evaluate_if_better(best_h, best_c, best_n, hF, best_n)

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = c5_max(best_h, best_n)

    if not np.isfinite(best_c):
        best_h = project_feasible(np.full(max(1, best_n), 0.5), best_n)
        best_c = c5_max(best_h, best_n)

    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
